Requirements\n   102→\n   103→- **neural**: Neural activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\n   104→  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\n   105→  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \n   106→  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\n   107→\n   108→- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\n   109→  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\n   110→  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\n   111→    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\n   112→    - audio: frequency, amplitude\n   113→    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\n   114→  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \n   115→  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\n   116→  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \n   117→\n   118→- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\n   119→  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n   120→  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n   121→  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n   122→  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n   123→  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n   124→\n   125→- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n   126→- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n   127→  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n   128→\n   129→- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n   130→- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n   131→\n   132→- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n   133→  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n   134→\n   135→- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n   136→  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n   137→- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n   138→  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n   139→\n   140→- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n   141→  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n   142→  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n   143→  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n   144→  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n   145→  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n   146→\n   147→## Decoder Reference\n   148→\n   149→- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n   150→- Optional arguments:\n   151→  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n   152→  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n   153→  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n   154→- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n   155→\n   156→### Purpose\n   157→\n   158→These functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n   159→1. Data structure is correct - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n   160→2. Dimensions are consistent - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n   161→3. Values are sensible - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n   162→4. Decoder can successfully train and predict\n   163→5. Poor performance indicates data formatting issues that need investigation\n   164→\n   165→### Consistency requirements\n   166→\n   167→Your processing and formatting must **match the reference paper and code** with respect to:\n   168→- Loading of data\n   169→- Temporal alignment of different time series streams (neural, inputs, outputs)\n   170→- Processing of neural, input, and output data streams\n   171→- Curation of data: filtering of low-quality neurons, trials, sessions, and mice.\n   172→\n   173→To check consistency, you must compare statistics available in the reference paper and your converted dataset. \n   174→\n   175→Whenever possible, invent **SANITY CHECKS** that your loading and processing matches the reference paper and code. \n   176→\n   177→Discrepancies are only allowed if required by the Decoder Input and Decoder Output specifications above. \n   178→\n   179→Carefully verify your work after every step. activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\n  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\n  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \n  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\n\n- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\n  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\n  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\n    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\n    - audio: frequency, amplitude\n    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\n  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \n  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\n  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\n  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n\n- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n\n- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n\n- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n\n- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n\n- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n\n## Decoder Reference\n\n- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n- Optional arguments:\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n\n### Purpose\n\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n1. Data structure is correct - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n2. Dimensions are consistent - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n3. Values are sensible - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n4. Decoder can successfully train and predict\n5. Poor performance indicates data formatting issues that need investigation\n\n### Consistency requirements\n\nYour processing and formatting must **match the reference paper and code** with respect to:\n- Loading of data\n- Temporal alignment of different time series streams (neural, inputs, outputs)\n- Processing of neural, input, and output data streams\n- Curation of data: filtering of low-quality neurons, trials, sessions, and mice.\n\nTo check consistency, you must compare statistics available in the reference paper and your converted dataset. \n\nWhenever possible, invent **SANITY CHECKS** that your loading and processing matches the reference paper and code. \n\nDiscrepancies are only allowed if required by the Decoder Input and Decoder Output specifications above. \n\nCarefully verify your work after every step. When you find mistakes, document and fix them immediately. Be critical of results **after every step of processing**. \n\nYou will be assessed on whether the decisions you make on how to load, filter, process, align, reformat, and save the data are reasonable and match the reference paper and code and these instructions.   41→\n    42→    The MATLAB code creates a gausswin(N), zeros the left half (making it causal),\n    43→    normalizes, then convolves with 'same'.\n    44→    \"\"\"\n    45→    if kernel_width_samples <= 1:\n    46→        return x\n    47→    N = kernel_width_samples\n    48→    # Create Gaussian window\n    49→    n = np.arange(N)\n    50→    center = (N - 1) / 2\n    51→    sigma = N / 6  # MATLAB gausswin default: alpha=2.5, sigma = (N-1)/(2*alpha)\n    52→    kern = np.exp(-0.5 * ((n - center) / sigma) ** 2)\n    53→    # Make causal: zero out left half\n    54→    kern[:N // 2] = 0\n    55→    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n    56→\n    57→    if x.ndim == 1:\n    58→        return np.convolve(x, kern, mode='same')\n    59→    else:\n    60→        out =      except:\n   425→                    feat_names = []\n   426→\n   427→                for trial_idx in range(n_traj_trials):\n   428→                    trial_data = {}\n   429→\n   430→                    # ts: (n_features, 3, n_frames) - MATLAB column-major stored in HDF5\n   431→                    try:\n   432→                        ts_ref = cam_group['ts'][trial_idx, 0]\n   433→                        ts = f[ts_ref][()]  # (n_features, 3, n_frames)\n   434→                        trial_data['ts'] = ts\n   435→                    except:\n   436→                        trial_data['ts'] = None\n   437→\n   438→                    # frameTimes\n   439→                    try:\n   440→                        ft_ref = cam_group['frameTimes'][trial_idx, 0]\n   441→                        ft = f[ft_ref][()].flatten()\n   442→                        trial_data['frameTimes'] = ft\n   443→                    except:\n   444→                        trial_data['frameTimes'] = None\n   445→\n   446→                    # NdroppedFrames\n   447→                    try:\n   448→                        ndf_ref = cam_group['NdroppedFrames'][trial_idx, 0]\n   449→                        ndf_val = f[ndf_ref][()].flatten()[0]\n   450→                        trial_data['NdroppedFrames'] = ndf_val\n   451→                    except:\n   452→                        trial_data['NdroppedFrames'] = 0\n   453→\n   454→                    cam_data.append(trial_data)\n   455→\n   456→                session['traj'].append({\n   457→                    'feat_names': feat_names,\n   458→                    'trials': cam_data\n   459→                })\n   460→\n   461→        # --- Video offset computation ---\n   462→        # vidshift = mode(sglx.bitcode.bitstart) / sglx.fs - mode(bp.ev.bitStart)\n   463→        try:\n   464→            bitStart_bp = np.nanmedian(ev['bitStart'][()].flatten())\n   465→            sglx = obj['sglx']\n   466→            fs = h5_read_scalar(sglx['fs'])\n   467→            bitstart_sglx = np.nanmedian(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart'][()].flatten())\n   468→            session['vidshift'] = bitstart_sglx / fs - bitStart_bp\n   469→        except:\n   470→            session['vidshift'] = 0.0\n   471→\n   472→    return session\n   473→\n   474→\n   475→def load_motion_energy(filepath):\n   476→    \"\"\"Load motion energy from .mat file (v5/v7 or v7.3/HDF5 format).\"\"\"\n   477→    try:\n   478→        # Try scipy first (v5/v7)\n   479→        mat = scipy.io.loadmat(filepath, squeeze_me=False)\n   480→        me = mat['me']\n   481→\n   482→        # Extract data and threshold\n   483→        data_field = me['data'][0, 0]\n   484→        thresh_field = me['moveThresh'][0, 0]\n   485→\n   486→        # data is an object array with per-trial arrays\n   487→        me_data = []\n   488→        for i in range(data_field.shape[0]):\n   489→            for j in range(data_field.shape[1]):\n   490→                trial_me = data_field[i, j].flatten().astype(float)\n   491→                me_data.append(trial_me)\n   492→\n   493→        threshold = float(thresh_field.flatten()[0])\n   494→        return {'data': me_data, 'moveThresh': threshold}\n   495→    except NotImplementedError:\n   496→        # v7.3 format - use h5py\n   497→        pass\n   498→    except Exception as e:\n   499→        # Try h5py as fallback\n   500→        pass\n   501→\n   502→    # Try HDF5 format\n   503→    try:\n   504→        with h5py.File(filepath, 'r') as f:\n   505→            me_group = f['me']\n   506→            # data is (n_trials, 1) array of refs\n   507→            data_refs = me_group['data'][()].flatten()\n   508→            me_data = []\n   509→            for ref in data_refs:\n   510→                trial_data = f[ref][()].flatten().astype(float)\n   511→                me_data.append(trial_data)\n   512→\n   513→            threshold = float(me_group['moveThresh'][()].flatten()[0])\n   514→            return {'data': me_data, 'moveThresh': threshold}\n   515→    except Exception as e:\n   516→        print(f\"  Warning: Could not load motion energy from {filepath}: {e}\")\n   517→        return None\n   518→\n   519→\n   520→def filter_clusters(clusters, quality_filter='all'):\n   521→    \"\"\"Filter clusters by quality, matching findClusters.m.\"\"\"\n   522→    excluded = {'garbage', 'gabrga', 'noisy', 'real?'}\n   523→    indices = []\n   524→    for i, unit in enumerate(clusters):\n   525→        q = unit.get('quality', '').strip().lower()\n   526→        if q not in excluded and q != '':\n   527→            indices.append(i)\n   528→        elif q == '':\n   529→            # Include unlabeled units (matching MATLAB 'all' behavior which includes empty strings)\n   530→            indices.append(i)\n   531→    return indices\n   532→\n   533→\n   534→def align_and_bin_spikes(clusters, cluster_indices, go_cue_times, trial_mask,\n   535→                         edges, dt, smooth_samples):\n   536→    \"\"\"Align spikes to goCue, bin, smooth, and compute firing rates.\n   537→\n   538→    Returns:\n   539→        trialdat: (n_timebins, n_units, n_trials) firing rate array\n   540→        mean_frs: (n_units,) mean firing rate per unit\n   541→    \"\"\"\n   542→    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0]\n   543→    n_trials = len(valid_trials)\n   544→    n_units = len(cluster_indices)\n   545→    n_timebins = len(edges) - 1\n   546→\n   547→    trialdat = np.zeros((n_timebins, n_units, n_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n   548→\n   549→    for ui, ci in enumerate(cluster_indices):\n   550→        unit = clusters[ci]\n   551→        spike_times = unit['trialtm']\n   552→        spike_trials = unit['trial']\n   553→\n   554→        for ti, trial_idx in enumerate(valid_trials):\n   555→            trial_num = trial_idx + 1  # MATLAB 1-indexed\n   556→            go_time = go_cue_times[trial_idx]\n   557→\n   558→            if np.isnan(go_time) or go_time == 0:\n   559→                continue\n   560→\n   561→            # Find spikes for this trial\n   562→            spike_mask = spike_trials == trial_num\n   563→            if not np.any(spike_mask):\n   564→                continue\n   565→\n   566→            # Align to go cue\n   567→            aligned_times = spike_times[spike_mask] - go_time\n   568→\n   569→            # Bin spikes\n   570→            counts, _ = np.histogram(aligned_times, bins=edges)\n   571→            counts = counts[:n_timebins].astype(float)\n   572→\n   573→            # Convert to firing rate and smooth\n   574→            fr = counts / dt\n   575→            fr_smooth = causal_gaussian_smooth(fr, smooth_samples)\n   576→            trialdat[:, ui, ti] = fr_smooth\n   577→\n   578→    # Compute mean firing rate per unit (across all trials and time)\n   579→    # Matching removeLowFRClusters.m: mean of mean across conditions\n   580→    # For simplicity, use mean across all time and trials\n   581→    mean_frs = np.mean(np.mean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=0)\n   582→\n   583→    return trialdat, mean_frs\n   584→\n   585→\n   586→def compute_velocity(positions, dt_video=1/400):\n   587→    \"\"\"Compute velocity magnitude from x,y positions.\n   588→\n   589→    positions: (n_frames, 2) array of [x, y]\n   590→    Returns: (n_frames,) velocity magnitude\n   591→    \"\"\"\n   592→    if positions is None or len(positions) < 2:\n   593→        return None\n   594→\n   595→    # Compute velocity as first derivative\n   596→    dx = np.gradient(positions[:, 0], dt_video)\n   597→    dy = np.gradient(positions[:, 1], dt_video)\n   598→    velocity = np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2)\n   599→    return velocity\n   600→\n   601→\n   602→def extract_kinematic_feature(session, cam_idx, feat_name, valid_trials, go_cue_times):\n   603→    \"\"\"Extract and interpolate a kinematic feature velocity to neural time axis.\n   604→\n   605→    ts from HDF5 has shape (n_features, 3, n_frames) where 3=[x, y, likelihood].\n   606→    For tongue features, positions are NaN when tongue is not visible.\n   607→\n   608→    Returns:\n   609→        velocities: (n_timebins, n_trials) array or None\n   610→    \"\"\"\n   611→    if cam_idx >= len(session['traj']):\n   612→        return None\n   613→\n   614→    cam = session['traj'][cam_idx]\n   615→    feat_names = cam['feat_names']\n   616→\n   617→    # Find exact feature index\n   618→    feat_idx = None\n   619→    for i, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n   620→        if fn.lower() == feat_name.lower():\n   621→            feat_idx = i\n   622→            break\n   623→\n   624→    if feat_idx is None:\n   625→        return None\n   626→\n   627→    is_tongue = 'tongue' in feat_name.lower()\n   628→    vidshift = session.get('vidshift', 0.0)\n   629→    n_trials = len(valid_trials)\n   630→    velocities = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   631→\n   632→    for ti, trial_idx in enumerate(valid_trials):\n   633→        if trial_idx >= len(cam['trials']):\n   634→            continue\n   635→\n   636→        trial_data = cam['trials'][trial_idx]\n   637→        ts = trial_data.get('ts')\n   638→        frame_times = trial_data.get('frameTimes')\n   639→\n   640→        if ts is None or ts.ndim != 3:\n   641→            continue\n   642→\n   643→        # Handle NdroppedFrames\n   644→        ndf = trial_data.get('NdroppedFrames', 0)\n   645→        if isinstance(ndf, float) and np.isnan(ndf):\n   646→            continue\n   647→\n   648→        go_time = go_cue_times[trial_idx]\n   649→        if np.isnan(go_time) or go_time == 0:\n   650→            continue\n   651→\n   652→        # ts shape: (n_features, 3, n_frames) from HDF5\n   653→        n_features, three, n_frames = ts.shape\n   654→        if three != 3 or feat_idx >= n_features:\n   655→            continue\n   656→\n   657→        # Extract x, y for the feature: (n_frames,) each\n   658→        x_pos = ts[feat_idx, 0, :].copy()\n   659→        y_pos = ts[feat_idx, 1, :].copy()\n   660→\n   661→        # Fill missing values for non-tongue features (matching MATLAB fillmissing nearest)\n   662→        if not is_tongue:\n   663→            for pos in [x_pos, y_pos]:\n   664→                mask = np.isnan(pos)\n   665→                if mask.any() and not mask.all():\n   666→                    valid_idx = np.where(~mask)[0]\n   667→                    pos[mask] = np.interp(np.where(mask)[0], valid_idx, pos[valid_idx])\n   668→\n   669→        # Compute velocity magnitude\n   670→        dt_vid = 1 / 400\n   671→        dx = np.gradient(x_pos, dt_vid)\n   672→        dy = np.gradient(y_pos, dt_vid)\n   673→        vel = np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2)\n   674→\n   675→        # For tongue, set velocity to 0 where tongue not visible\n   676→        if is_tongue:\n   677→            nan_mask = np.isnan(x_pos) | np.isnan(y_pos)\n   678→            vel[nan_mask] = 0.0\n   679→            vel[np.isnan(vel)] = 0.0\n   680→\n   681→        # Create frame times if not available\n   682→        if frame_times is None or np.all(np.isnan(frame_times)):\n   683→            frame_times = np.arange(1, n_frames + 1) / 400.0\n   684→\n   685→        # Align to go cue and interpolate to neural time axis\n   686→        aligned_frame_times = frame_times[:n_frames] - vidshift - go_time\n   687→\n   688→        try:\n   689→            interp_fn = interp1d(aligned_frame_times, vel,\n   690→                                kind='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n   691→            velocities[:, ti] = interp_fn(TIME_AXIS)\n   692→        except:\n   693→            continue\n   694→\n   695→    # Fill NaN with nearest valid values (or 0 for tongue)\n   696→    for ti in range(n_trials):\n   697→        col = velocities[:, ti]\n   698→        mask = np.isnan(col)\n   699→        if mask.any() and not mask.all():\n   700→            valid_idx = np.where(~mask)[0]\n   701→            col[mask] = np.interp(np.where(mask)[0], valid_idx, col[valid_idx])\n   702→            velocities[:, ti] = col\n   703→        elif mask.all():\n   704→            velocities[:, ti] = 0.0\n   705→\n   706→    return velocities\n   707→\n   708→\n   709→def interpolate_motion_energy(me_data, session, valid_trials, go_cue_times):\n   710→    \"\"\"Interpolate motion energy to neural time axis, aligned to go cue.\n   711→\n   712→    Matching loadMotionEnergy.m logic.\n   713→    \"\"\"\n   714→    vidshift = session.get('vidshift', 0.0)\n   715→    n_trials = len(valid_trials)\n   716→    me_interp = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   717→\n   718→    for ti, trial_idx in enumerate(valid_trials):\n   719→        if trial_idx >= len(me_data):\n   720→            continue\n   721→\n   722→        me_trial = me_data[trial_idx]\n   723→        go_time = go_cue_times[trial_idx]\n   724→\n   725→        if np.isnan(go_time) or go_time == 0:\n   726→            continue\n   727→\n   728→        if me_trial is None or len(me_trial) == 0:\n   729→            continue\n   730→\n   731→        n_frames = len(me_trial)\n   732→\n   733→        # Get frame times\n   734→        # Try to use traj frameTimes, otherwise fall back\n   735→        frame_times = None\n   736→        if len(session['traj']) > 0 and trial_idx < len(session['traj'][0]['trials']):\n   737→            ft = session['traj'][0]['trials'][trial_idx].get('frameTimes')\n   738→            if ft is not None and not np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n   739→                frame_times = ft\n   740→\n   741→        if frame_times is None:\n   742→            frame_times = np.arange(1, n_frames + 1) / 400.0\n   743→            aligned_frame_times = frame_times - 0.5 - go_time  # fallback offset\n   744→        else:\n   745→            # Trim/pad to match me_trial length\n   746→            if len(frame_times) > n_frames:\n   747→                frame_times = frame_times[:n_frames]\n   748→            elif len(frame_times) < n_frames:\n   749→                # Extend frame times\n   750→                dt_vid = 1/400\n   751→                extra = np.arange(len(frame_times), n_frames) * dt_vid + frame_times[-1] + dt_vid\n   752→                frame_times = np.concatenate([frame_times, extra])\n   753→            aligned_frame_times = frame_times - vidshift - go_time\n   754→\n   755→        try:\n   756→            interp_fn = interp1d(aligned_frame_times, me_trial,\n   757→                                kind='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n   758→            me_interp[:, ti] = interp_fn(TIME_AXIS)\n   759→        except:\n   760→            continue\n   761→\n   762→    # Fill NaN with nearest\n   763→    for ti in range(n_trials):\n   764→        col = me_interp[:, ti]\n   765→        mask = np.isnan(col)\n   766→        if mask.any() and not mask.all():\n   767→            valid_idx = np.where(~mask)[0]\n   768→            col[mask] = np.interp(np.where(mask)[0], valid_idx, col[valid_idx])\n   769→            me_interp[:, ti] = col\n   770→        elif mask.all():\n   771→            me_interp[:, ti] = 0.0\n   772→\n   773→    return me_interp\n   774→\n   775→\n   776→def discretize_per_session(values, percentile=50):\n   777→    \"\"\"Discretize values into 2 bins using per-session threshold.\n   778→\n   779→    values: (n_timebins, n_trials)\n   780→    Returns: (n_timebins, n_trials) with 0 (< threshold) and 1 (>= threshold)\n   781→\n   782→    The threshold is the 50th percentile (median). Per the decoder task spec:\n   783→    0: < 50th percentile, 1: >= 50th percentile.\n   784→    \"\"\"\n   785→    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n   786→    if len(valid) == 0:\n   787→        return np.zeros_like(values, dtype=np.int32)\n   788→\n   789→    threshold = np.percentile(valid, percentile)\n   790→    discretized = (values >= threshold).astype(np.int32)\n   791→    return discretized\n   792→\n   793→\n   794→def process_session(session_file, me_file, session_idx_global):\n   795→    \"\"\"Process a single session and return formatted data.\"\"\"\n   796→\n   797→    basename = os.path.basename(session_file)\n   798→    parts = basename.replace('data_structure_', '').replace('.mat', '').split('_')\n   799→    animal = parts[0]\n   800→    date = '_'.join(parts[1:])\n   801→\n   802→    print(f\"\\n--- Processing session {session_idx_global}: {animal}_{date} ---\")\n   803→\n   804→    # Load session data\n   805→    session = load_session_data(session_file)\n   806→    Ntrials = session['Ntrials']\n   807→    print(f\"  Total trials: {Ntrials}\")\n   808→\n   809→    # Load motion energy\n   810→    me = load_motion_energy(me_file) if me_file else None\n   811→\n   812→    # --- Trial filtering ---\n   813→    # Include: non-stim, non-early, responding (hit or miss) trials\n   814→    # Matching: ~stim.enable & ~early & (hit | miss)\n   815→    trial_mask = (\n   816→        (session['stim_enable'] == 0) &\n   817→        (session['early'] == 0) &\n   818→        ((session['hit'] == 1) | (session['miss'] == 1))\n   819→    )\n   820→\n   821→    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0]\n   822→    n_valid = len(valid_trials)\n   823→    print(f\"  Valid trials (no stim, no early, hit|miss): {n_valid}\")\n   824→\n   825→    if n_valid < 2:\n   826→        print(f\"  SKIPPING: fewer than 2 valid trials\")\n   827→        return None\n   828→\n   829→    # --- Cluster filtering and spike processing ---\n   830→    # Process all probes\n   831→    all_cluster_indices = []\n   832→    all_clusters = []\n   833→    probe_region_labels = []\n   834→\n   835→    for probe_idx in range(session['n_probes']):\n   836→        clusters = session['clusters'][probe_idx]\n   837→\n   838→        # Get probe location\n   839→        if probe_idx < len(session['probe_locations']):\n   840→            loc = session['probe_locations'][probe_idx]\n   841→        else:\n   842→            loc = 'ALM'\n   843→\n   844→        # Only include ALM probes (paper: \"We recorded activity extracellularly in the ALM\")\n   845→        loc_upper = loc.strip().upper()\n   846→        if 'ALM' not in loc_upper:\n   847→            print(f\"  Probe {probe_idx} ({loc}): skipping non-ALM probe\")\n   848→            continue\n   849→\n   850→        # Filter by quality (matching findClusters.m with quality='all')\n   851→        valid_units = filter_clusters(clusters)\n   852→\n   853→        if len(valid_units) == 0:\n   854→            continue\n   855→\n   856→        # Compute firing rates for this probe\n   857→        trialdat_probe, mean_frs = align_and_bin_spikes(\n   858→            clusters, valid_units, session['goCue'], trial_mask,\n   859→            EDGES, DT, SMOOTH_MS\n   860→        )\n   861→\n   862→        # Filter by firing rate (matching removeLowFRClusters.m)\n   863→        fr_mask = mean_frs > LOW_FR\n   864→        kept_units = [valid_units[i] for i in range(len(valid_units)) if fr_mask[i]]\n   865→        trialdat_probe = trialdat_probe[:, fr_mask, :]\n   866→\n   867→        print(f\"  Probe {probe_idx} ({loc}): {len(clusters)} total -> {len(valid_units)} quality-filtered -> {len(kept_units)} FR-filtered units\")\n   868→\n   869→        all_clusters.append(trialdat_probe)\n   870→        for _ in kept_units:\n   871→            probe_region_labels.append(loc)\n   872→\n   873→    if len(all_clusters) == 0:\n   874→        print(f\"  SKIPPING: no valid clusters\")\n   875→        return None\n   876→\n   877→    # Concatenate across probes: (n_timebins, n_total_units, n_trials)\n   878→    neural_data = np.concatenate(all_clusters, axis=1)\n   879→    n_units = neural_data.shape[1]\n   880→    n_trials = neural_data.shape[2]\n   881→    print(f\"  Neural data shape: {neural_data.shape} (time, units, trials)\")\n   882→\n   883→    if n_units == 0:\n   884→        print(f\"  SKIPPING: 0 valid units after filtering\")\n   885→        return None\n   886→\n   887→    # --- Extract behavioral variables ---\n   888→    lick_direction = session['R'][valid_trials].astype(np.int32)  # R=1, L=0\n   889→    context = (1 - session['autowater'][valid_trials]).astype(np.int32)  # DR=1, WC=0\n   890→    outcome = session['hit'][valid_trials].astype(np.int32)  # correct=1, incorrect=0\n   891→\n   892→    print(f\"  Lick direction: R={np.sum(lick_direction==1)}, L={np.sum(lick_direction==0)}\")\n   893→    print(f\"  Context: DR={np.sum(context==1)}, WC={np.sum(context==0)}\")\n   894→    print(f\"  Outcome: correct={np.sum(outcome==1)}, incorrect={np.sum(outcome==0)}\")\n   895→\n   896→    # --- Extract kinematic features ---\n   897→    go_cue_times = session['goCue']\n   898→\n   899→    # Tongue velocity from side camera (cam 0), feature 'tongue'\n   900→    tongue_vel = extract_kinematic_feature(session, 0, 'tongue', valid_trials, go_cue_times)\n   901→\n   902→    # Paw velocity from bottom camera (cam 1)\n   903→    # Average top_paw and bottom_paw velocities when both available\n   904→    paw_vel = None\n   905→    if len(session['traj']) > 1:\n   906→        paw_top = extract_kinematic_feature(session, 1, 'top_paw', valid_trials, go_cue_times)\n   907→        paw_bot = extract_kinematic_feature(session, 1, 'bottom_paw', valid_trials, go_cue_times)\n   908→\n   909→        if paw_top is not None and paw_bot is not None:\n   910→            paw_vel = (paw_top + paw_bot) / 2.0\n   911→        elif paw_top is not None:\n   912→            paw_vel = paw_top\n   913→        elif paw_bot is not None:\n   914→            paw_vel = paw_bot\n   915→\n   916→    # gausswin(N), zeros the left half (making it causal),\n    normalizes, then convolves with 'same'.\n    \"\"\"\n    if kernel_width_samples <= 1:\n        return x\n    N = kernel_width_samples\n    # sigma) ** 2)\n    # Make causal: zero out left half\n    kern[:N // 2] = 0\n    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n\n    if x.ndim == 1:\n        return np.convolve(x, kern, mode='same')\n    else:\n probe_group:\n                    session['clusters'].append([])\n                    continue\n\n                n_units = probe_group['quality'].shape[0]\n\n                probe_clusters = []\n                for unit_idx in range(n_units):\n                    unit = {}\n\n                    # Quality\n                    q_ref = probe_group['quality'][unit_idx, 0]\n                    try:\n                        unit['quality'] = h5_read_string(f, f[q_ref])\n                    except:\n                        unit['quality'] = ''\n\n                    # Spike times (within trial, relative to trial start)\n                    tm_ref = probe_group['trialtm'][unit_idx, 0]\n                    unit['trialtm'] = f[tm_ref][()].flatten()\n\n                    # Trial assignment for each spike\n                    trial_ref = probe_group['trial'][unit_idx, 0]\n                    unit['trial'] = f[trial_ref][()].flatten().astype(int)\n\n                    probe_clusters.append(unit)\n\n                session['clusters'].append(probe_clusters)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"  Warning: Could not load probe {probe_idx}: {e}\")\n                session['clusters'].append([])\n\n        # --- Probe location info ---\n        session['probe_locations'] = []\n        if 'meta' in obj and isinstance(obj['meta'], h5py.Group) and 'probe' in obj['meta']:\n            probe_meta = obj['meta']['probe']\n            if 'loc' in probe_meta:\n                loc_data = probe_meta['loc'][()].flatten()\n                for loc_ref in loc_data:\n                    try:\n                        loc_str = h5_read_string(f, f[loc_ref])\n                        session['probe_locations'].append(loc_str)\n                    except:\n                        session['probe_locations'].append('ALM')\n\n        # --- Trajectory data for kinematics ---\n        session['traj'] = []\n        if 'traj' in obj:\n            traj_refs = obj['traj'][()].flatten()\n            for cam_idx in range(len(traj_refs)):\n                cam_group = f[traj_refs[cam_idx]]\n                cam_data = []\n\n                # Each camera has per-trial data\n                n_traj_trials = cam_group['ts'].shape[0]\n\n                # Get feature names from first trial\n                # featNames is (n_trials, 1) refs, each ref -> dataset of (1, n_feats) refs\n                feat_names = []\n                try:\n                    fn_ref = cam_group['featNames'][0, 0]\n                    fn_dataset = f[fn_ref][()]  # (1, n_feats) array of refs\n                    for ref in fn_dataset.flatten():\n                        feat_names.append(h5_read_string(f, f[ref]))\n                except:\n                    feat_names = []\n\n                for trial_idx in range(n_traj_trials):\n                    trial_data = {}\n\n                    # ts: (n_features, 3, n_frames) - MATLAB column-major stored in HDF5\n                    try:\n                        ts_ref = cam_group['ts'][trial_idx, 0]\n                        ts = f[ts_ref][()]  # (n_features, 3, n_frames)\n                        trial_data['ts'] = ts\n                    except:\n                        trial_data['ts'] = None\n\n                    # frameTimes\n                    try:\n                        ft_ref = cam_group['frameTimes'][trial_idx, 0]\n                        ft = f[ft_ref][()].flatten()\n                        trial_data['frameTimes'] = ft\n                    except:\n                        trial_data['frameTimes'] = None\n\n                    # NdroppedFrames\n                    try:\n                        ndf_ref = cam_group['NdroppedFrames'][trial_idx, 0]\n                        ndf_val = f[ndf_ref][()].flatten()[0]\n                        trial_data['NdroppedFrames'] = ndf_val\n                    except:\n                        trial_data['NdroppedFrames'] = 0\n\n                    cam_data.append(trial_data)\n\n                session['traj'].append({\n                    'feat_names': feat_names,\n                    'trials': cam_data\n                })\n\n        # --- Video offset computation ---\n        # vidshift = mode(sglx.bitcode.bitstart) / sglx.fs - mode(bp.ev.bitStart)\n        try:\n            bitStart_bp = np.nanmedian(ev['bitStart'][()].flatten())\n            sglx = obj['sglx']\n            fs = h5_read_scalar(sglx['fs'])\n            bitstart_sglx = np.nanmedian(sglx['bitcode']['bitstart'][()].flatten())\n            session['vidshift'] = bitstart_sglx / fs - bitStart_bp\n        except:\n            session['vidshift'] = 0.0\n\n    return session\n\n\ndef load_motion_energy(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load motion energy from .mat file (v5/v7 or v7.3/HDF5 format).\"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Try scipy first (v5/v7)\n        mat = scipy.io.loadmat(filepath, squeeze_me=False)\n        me = mat['me']\n\n        # Extract data and threshold\n        data_field = me['data'][0, 0]\n        thresh_field = me['moveThresh'][0, 0]\n\n        # data is an object array with per-trial arrays\n        me_data = []\n        for i in range(data_field.shape[0]):\n            for j in range(data_field.shape[1]):\n                trial_me = data_field[i, j].flatten().astype(float)\n                me_data.append(trial_me)\n\n        threshold = float(thresh_field.flatten()[0])\n        return {'data': me_data, 'moveThresh': threshold}\n    except NotImplementedError:\n        # v7.3 format - use h5py\n        pass\n    except Exception as e:\n        # Try h5py as fallback\n        pass\n\n    # Try HDF5 format\n    try:\n        with h5py.File(filepath, 'r') as f:\n            me_group = f['me']\n            # data is (n_trials, 1) array of refs\n            data_refs = me_group['data'][()].flatten()\n            me_data = []\n            for ref in data_refs:\n                trial_data = f[ref][()].flatten().astype(float)\n                me_data.append(trial_data)\n\n            threshold = float(me_group['moveThresh'][()].flatten()[0])\n            return {'data': me_data, 'moveThresh': threshold}\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Warning: Could not load motion energy from {filepath}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef filter_clusters(clusters, quality_filter='all'):\n    \"\"\"Filter clusters by quality, matching findClusters.m.\"\"\"\n    excluded = {'garbage', 'gabrga', 'noisy', 'real?'}\n    indices = []\n    for i, unit in enumerate(clusters):\n        q = unit.get('quality', '').strip().lower()\n        if q not in excluded and q != '':\n            indices.append(i)\n        elif q == '':\n            # Include unlabeled units (matching MATLAB 'all' behavior which includes empty strings)\n            indices.append(i)\n    return indices\n\n\ndef align_and_bin_spikes(clusters, cluster_indices, go_cue_times, trial_mask,\n                         edges, dt, smooth_samples):\n    \"\"\"Align spikes to goCue, bin, smooth, and compute firing rates.\n\n    Returns:\n        trialdat: (n_timebins, n_units, n_trials) firing rate array\n        mean_frs: (n_units,) mean firing rate per unit\n    \"\"\"\n    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0]\n    n_trials = len(valid_trials)\n    n_units = len(cluster_indices)\n    n_timebins = len(edges) - 1\n\n    trialdat = np.zeros((n_timebins, n_units, n_trials), dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for ui, ci in enumerate(cluster_indices):\n        unit = clusters[ci]\n        spike_times = unit['trialtm']\n        spike_trials = unit['trial']\n\n        for ti, trial_idx in enumerate(valid_trials):\n            trial_num = trial_idx + 1  # MATLAB 1-indexed\n            go_time = go_cue_times[trial_idx]\n\n            if np.isnan(go_time) or go_time == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Find spikes for this trial\n            spike_mask = spike_trials == trial_num\n            if not np.any(spike_mask):\n                continue\n\n            # Align to go cue\n            aligned_times = spike_times[spike_mask] - go_time\n\n            # Bin spikes\n            counts, _ = np.histogram(aligned_times, bins=edges)\n            counts = counts[:n_timebins].astype(float)\n\n            # Convert to firing rate and smooth\n            fr = counts / dt\n            fr_smooth = causal_gaussian_smooth(fr, smooth_samples)\n            trialdat[:, ui, ti] = fr_smooth\n\n    # Compute mean firing rate per unit (across all trials and time)\n    # Matching removeLowFRClusters.m: mean of mean across conditions\n    # For simplicity, use mean across all time and trials\n    mean_frs = np.mean(np.mean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=0)\n\n    return trialdat, mean_frs\n\n\ndef compute_velocity(positions, dt_video=1/400):\n    \"\"\"Compute velocity magnitude from x,y positions.\n\n    positions: (n_frames, 2) array of [x, y]\n    Returns: (n_frames,) velocity magnitude\n    \"\"\"\n    if positions is None or len(positions) < 2:\n        return None\n\n    # Compute velocity as first derivative\n    dx = np.gradient(positions[:, 0], dt_video)\n    dy = np.gradient(positions[:, 1], dt_video)\n    velocity = np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2)\n    return velocity\n\n\ndef extract_kinematic_feature(session, cam_idx, feat_name, valid_trials, go_cue_times):\n    \"\"\"Extract and interpolate a kinematic feature velocity to neural time axis.\n\n    ts from HDF5 has shape (n_features, 3, n_frames) where 3=[x, y, likelihood].\n    For tongue features, positions are NaN when tongue is not visible.\n\n    Returns:\n        velocities: (n_timebins, n_trials) array or None\n    \"\"\"\n    if cam_idx >= len(session['traj']):\n        return None\n\n    cam = session['traj'][cam_idx]\n    feat_names = cam['feat_names']\n\n    # Find exact feature index\n    feat_idx = None\n    for i, fn in enumerate(feat_names):\n        if fn.lower() == feat_name.lower():\n            feat_idx = i\n            break\n\n    if feat_idx is None:\n        return None\n\n    is_tongue = 'tongue' in feat_name.lower()\n    vidshift = session.get('vidshift', 0.0)\n    n_trials = len(valid_trials)\n    velocities = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for ti, trial_idx in enumerate(valid_trials):\n        if trial_idx >= len(cam['trials']):\n            continue\n\n        trial_data = cam['trials'][trial_idx]\n        ts = trial_data.get('ts')\n        frame_times = trial_data.get('frameTimes')\n\n        if ts is None or ts.ndim != 3:\n            continue\n\n        # Handle NdroppedFrames\n        ndf = trial_data.get('NdroppedFrames', 0)\n        if isinstance(ndf, float) and np.isnan(ndf):\n            continue\n\n        go_time = go_cue_times[trial_idx]\n        if np.isnan(go_time) or go_time == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # ts shape: (n_features, 3, n_frames) from HDF5\n        n_features, three, n_frames = ts.shape\n        if three != 3 or feat_idx >= n_features:\n            continue\n\n        # Extract x, y for the feature: (n_frames,) each\n        x_pos = ts[feat_idx, 0, :].copy()\n        y_pos = ts[feat_idx, 1, :].copy()\n\n        # Fill missing values for non-tongue features (matching MATLAB fillmissing nearest)\n        if not is_tongue:\n            for pos in [x_pos, y_pos]:\n                mask = np.isnan(pos)\n                if mask.any() and not mask.all():\n                    valid_idx = np.where(~mask)[0]\n                    pos[mask] = np.interp(np.where(mask)[0], valid_idx, pos[valid_idx])\n\n        # Compute velocity magnitude\n        dt_vid = 1 / 400\n        dx = np.gradient(x_pos, dt_vid)\n        dy = np.gradient(y_pos, dt_vid)\n        vel = np.sqrt(dx**2 + dy**2)\n\n        # For tongue, set velocity to 0 where tongue not visible\n        if is_tongue:\n            nan_mask = np.isnan(x_pos) | np.isnan(y_pos)\n            vel[nan_mask] = 0.0\n            vel[np.isnan(vel)] = 0.0\n\n        # Create frame times if not available\n        if frame_times is None or np.all(np.isnan(frame_times)):\n            frame_times = np.arange(1, n_frames + 1) / 400.0\n\n        # Align to go cue and interpolate to neural time axis\n        aligned_frame_times = frame_times[:n_frames] - vidshift - go_time\n\n        try:\n            interp_fn = interp1d(aligned_frame_times, vel,\n                                kind='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n            velocities[:, ti] = interp_fn(TIME_AXIS)\n        except:\n            continue\n\n    # Fill NaN with nearest valid values (or 0 for tongue)\n    for ti in range(n_trials):\n        col = velocities[:, ti]\n        mask = np.isnan(col)\n        if mask.any() and not mask.all():\n            valid_idx = np.where(~mask)[0]\n            col[mask] = np.interp(np.where(mask)[0], valid_idx, col[valid_idx])\n            velocities[:, ti] = col\n        elif mask.all():\n            velocities[:, ti] = 0.0\n\n    return velocities\n\n\ndef interpolate_motion_energy(me_data, session, valid_trials, go_cue_times):\n    \"\"\"Interpolate motion energy to neural time axis, aligned to go cue.\n\n    Matching loadMotionEnergy.m logic.\n    \"\"\"\n    vidshift = session.get('vidshift', 0.0)\n    n_trials = len(valid_trials)\n    me_interp = np.full((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for ti, trial_idx in enumerate(valid_trials):\n        if trial_idx >= len(me_data):\n            continue\n\n        me_trial = me_data[trial_idx]\n        go_time = go_cue_times[trial_idx]\n\n        if np.isnan(go_time) or go_time == 0:\n            continue\n\n        if me_trial is None or len(me_trial) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        n_frames = len(me_trial)\n\n        # Get frame times\n        # Try to use traj frameTimes, otherwise fall back\n        frame_times = None\n        if len(session['traj']) > 0 and trial_idx < len(session['traj'][0]['trials']):\n            ft = session['traj'][0]['trials'][trial_idx].get('frameTimes')\n            if ft is not None and not np.all(np.isnan(ft)):\n                frame_times = ft\n\n        if frame_times is None:\n            frame_times = np.arange(1, n_frames + 1) / 400.0\n            aligned_frame_times = frame_times - 0.5 - go_time  # fallback offset\n        else:\n            # Trim/pad to match me_trial length\n            if len(frame_times) > n_frames:\n                frame_times = frame_times[:n_frames]\n            elif len(frame_times) < n_frames:\n                # Extend frame times\n                dt_vid = 1/400\n                extra = np.arange(len(frame_times), n_frames) * dt_vid + frame_times[-1] + dt_vid\n                frame_times = np.concatenate([frame_times, extra])\n            aligned_frame_times = frame_times - vidshift - go_time\n\n        try:\n            interp_fn = interp1d(aligned_frame_times, me_trial,\n                                kind='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n            me_interp[:, ti] = interp_fn(TIME_AXIS)\n        except:\n            continue\n\n    # Fill NaN with nearest\n    for ti in range(n_trials):\n        col = me_interp[:, ti]\n        mask = np.isnan(col)\n        if mask.any() and not mask.all():\n            valid_idx = np.where(~mask)[0]\n            col[mask] = np.interp(np.where(mask)[0], valid_idx, col[valid_idx])\n            me_interp[:, ti] = col\n        elif mask.all():\n            me_interp[:, ti] = 0.0\n\n    return me_interp\n\n\ndef discretize_per_session(values, percentile=50):\n    \"\"\"Discretize values into 2 bins using per-session threshold.\n\n    values: (n_timebins, n_trials)\n    Returns: (n_timebins, n_trials) with 0 (< threshold) and 1 (>= threshold)\n\n    The threshold is the 50th percentile (median). Per the decoder task spec:\n    0: < 50th percentile, 1: >= 50th percentile.\n    \"\"\"\n    valid = values[~np.isnan(values)]\n    if len(valid) == 0:\n        return np.zeros_like(values, dtype=np.int32)\n\n    threshold = np.percentile(valid, percentile)\n    discretized = (values >= threshold).astype(np.int32)\n    return discretized\n\n\ndef process_session(session_file, me_file, session_idx_global):\n    \"\"\"Process a single session and return formatted data.\"\"\"\n\n    basename = os.path.basename(session_file)\n    parts = basename.replace('data_structure_', '').replace('.mat', '').split('_')\n    animal = parts[0]\n    date = '_'.join(parts[1:])\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- Processing session {session_idx_global}: {animal}_{date} ---\")\n\n    # Load session data\n    session = load_session_data(session_file)\n    Ntrials = session['Ntrials']\n    print(f\"  Total trials: {Ntrials}\")\n\n    # Load motion energy\n    me = load_motion_energy(me_file) if me_file else None\n\n    # --- Trial filtering ---\n    # Include: non-stim, non-early, responding (hit or miss) trials\n    # Matching: ~stim.enable & ~early & (hit | miss)\n    trial_mask = (\n        (session['stim_enable'] == 0) &\n        (session['early'] == 0) &\n        ((session['hit'] == 1) | (session['miss'] == 1))\n    )\n\n    valid_trials = np.where(trial_mask)[0]\n    n_valid = len(valid_trials)\n    print(f\"  Valid trials (no stim, no early, hit|miss): {n_valid}\")\n\n    if n_valid < 2:\n        print(f\"  SKIPPING: fewer than 2 valid trials\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Cluster filtering and spike processing ---\n    # Process all probes\n    all_cluster_indices = []\n    all_clusters = []\n    probe_region_labels = []\n\n    for probe_idx in range(session['n_probes']):\n        clusters = session['clusters'][probe_idx]\n\n        # Get probe location\n        if probe_idx < len(session['probe_locations']):\n            loc = session['probe_locations'][probe_idx]\n        else:\n            loc = 'ALM'\n\n        # Only include ALM probes (paper: \"We recorded activity extracellularly in the ALM\")\n        loc_upper = loc.strip().upper()\n        if 'ALM' not in loc_upper:\n            print(f\"  Probe {probe_idx} ({loc}): skipping non-ALM probe\")\n            continue\n\n        # Filter by quality (matching findClusters.m with quality='all')\n        valid_units = filter_clusters(clusters)\n\n        if len(valid_units) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Compute firing rates for this probe\n        trialdat_probe, mean_frs = align_and_bin_spikes(\n            clusters, valid_units, session['goCue'], trial_mask,\n            EDGES, DT, SMOOTH_MS\n        )\n\n        # Filter by firing rate (matching removeLowFRClusters.m)\n        fr_mask = mean_frs > LOW_FR\n        kept_units = [valid_units[i] for i in range(len(valid_units)) if fr_mask[i]]\n        trialdat_probe = trialdat_probe[:, fr_mask, :]\n\n        print(f\"  Probe {probe_idx} ({loc}): {len(clusters)} total -> {len(valid_units)} quality-filtered -> {len(kept_units)} FR-filtered units\")\n\n        all_clusters.append(trialdat_probe)\n        for _ in kept_units:\n            probe_region_labels.append(loc)\n\n    if len(all_clusters) == 0:\n        print(f\"  SKIPPING: no valid clusters\")\n        return None\n\n    # Concatenate across probes: (n_timebins, n_total_units, n_trials)\n    neural_data = np.concatenate(all_clusters, axis=1)\n    n_units = neural_data.shape[1]\n    n_trials = neural_data.shape[2]\n    print(f\"  Neural data shape: {neural_data.shape} (time, units, trials)\")\n\n    if n_units == 0:\n        print(f\"  SKIPPING: 0 valid units after filtering\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Extract behavioral variables ---\n    lick_direction = session['R'][valid_trials].astype(np.int32)  # R=1, L=0\n    context = (1 - session['autowater'][valid_trials]).astype(np.int32)  # DR=1, WC=0\n    outcome = session['hit'][valid_trials].astype(np.int32)  # correct=1, incorrect=0\n\n    print(f\"  Lick direction: R={np.sum(lick_direction==1)}, L={np.sum(lick_direction==0)}\")\n    print(f\"  Context: DR={np.sum(context==1)}, WC={np.sum(context==0)}\")\n    print(f\"  Outcome: correct={np.sum(outcome==1)}, incorrect={np.sum(outcome==0)}\")\n\n    # --- Extract kinematic features ---\n    go_cue_times = session['goCue']\n\n    # Tongue velocity from side camera (cam 0), feature 'tongue'\n    tongue_vel = extract_kinematic_feature(session, 0, 'tongue', valid_trials, go_cue_times)\n\n    # Paw velocity from bottom camera (cam 1)\n    # Average top_paw and bottom_paw velocities when both available\n    paw_vel = None\n    if len(session['traj']) > 1:\n        paw_top = extract_kinematic_feature(session, 1, 'top_paw', valid_trials, go_cue_times)\n        paw_bot = extract_kinematic_feature(session, 1, 'bottom_paw', valid_trials, go_cue_times)\n\n        if paw_top is not None and paw_bot is not None:\n            paw_vel = (paw_top + paw_bot) / 2.0\n        elif paw_top is not None:\n            paw_vel = paw_top\n        elif paw_bot is not None:\n            paw_vel = paw_bot\n\n    # Motion energy\n    me_interp = None\n    if me is not None:\n        me_interp = interpolate_motion_energy(me['data'], session, valid_trials, go_cue_times)\n\n    # --- Discretize continuous outputs ---\n    if tongue_vel is not None:\n        tongue_vel_disc = discretize_per_session(tongue_vel)\n    else:\n        print(f\"  Warning: No tongue velocity data, using zeros\")\n        tongue_vel_disc = np.zeros((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), dtype=np.int32)\n\n    if paw_vel is not None:\n        paw_vel_disc = discretize_per_session(paw_vel)\n    else:\n        print(f\"  Warning: No paw velocity data, using zeros\")\n        paw_vel_disc = np.zeros((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), dtype=np.int32)\n\n    if me_interp is not None:\n        me_disc = discretize_per_session(me_interp)\n    else:\n        print(f\"  Warning: No motion energy data, using zeros\")\n        me_disc = np.zeros((N_TIMEBINS, n_trials), dtype=np.int32)\n\n    # --- Format per-trial data ---\n    neural_trials = []\n    input_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n\n    for ti in range(n_trials):\n        # Neural: (n_units, n_timebins)\n        neural_trial = neural_data[:, :, ti].T.astype(np.float32)\n        neural_trials.append(neural_trial)\n\n        # Input: time from go cue (1, n_timebins)\n        input_trial = TIME_AXIS.reshape(1, -1).astype(np.float32)\n        input_trials.append(input_trial)\n\n        # Output: (6, n_timebins) - per-trial values tiled, time-varying ones as-is\n        output_trial = np.zeros((6, N_TIMEBINS), dtype=np.int32)\n        output_trial[0, :] = lick_direction[ti]  # per-trial\n        output_trial[1, :] = context[ti]  # per-trial\n        output_trial[2, :] = outcome[ti]  # per-trial\n        output_trial[3, :] = tongue_vel_disc[:, ti]  # time-varying\n        output_trial[4, :] = paw_vel_disc[:, ti]  # time-varying\n        output_trial[5, :] = me_disc[:, ti]  # time-varying\n        output_trials.append(output_trial)\n\n    # --- Brain region info ---\n    # Clean up region labels\n    region_labels_clean = []\n    for label in probe_region_labels:\n        label_clean = label.strip()\n        # Normalize common variations\n        if 'ALM' in label_clean.upper():\n            label_clean = 'ALM'\n        elif 'TJM1' in label_clean.upper() or 'M1' in label_clean.upper():\n            label_clean = 'tjM1'\n        region_labels_clean.append(label_clean)\n\n    return {\n        'neural': neural_trials,\n        'input': input_trials,\n        'output': output_trials,\n        'animal': animal,\n        'date': date,\n        'region_labels': region_labels_clean,\n        'n_units': n_units,\n        'n_trials': n_trials,\n    }\n\n\ndef find_session_files(data_dirs):\n    \"\"\"Find all data_structure and motionEnergy file pairs.\"\"\"\n    sessions = []\n\n    for data_dir in data_dirs:\n        if not os.path.isdir(data_dir):\n            continue\n\n        data_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(data_dir, 'data_structure_*.mat')))\n\n        for df in data_files:\n            basename = os.path.basename(df)\n            # Extract animal_date from data_structure_ANIMAL_DATE.mat\n            parts = basename.replace('data_structure_', '').replace('.mat', '')\n            me_file = os.path.join(data_dir, f'motionEnergy_{parts}.mat')\n\n            if not os.path.exists(me_file):\n                me_file = None\n\n            sessions.append({\n                'data_file': df,\n                'me_file': me_file,\n                'animal_date': parts,\n            })\n\n    return sessions\n\n\ndef convert_data(data_dirs=DATA_DIRS, max_sessions=None, output_file='converted_data.pkl'):\n    \"\"\"Main conversion function.\"\"\"\n\n    # Find all session files\n    session_files = find_session_files(data_dirs)\n    print(f\"Found {len(session_files)} sessions\")\n\n    if max_sessions is not None:\n        session_files = session_files[:max_sessions]\n        print(f\"Processing first {max_sessions} sessions\")\n\n    # Process each session\n    all_neural = []\n    all_input = []\n    all_output = []\n    all_animals = []\n    all_dates = []\n    all_region_labels = []\n\n    for idx, sf in enumerate(session_files):\n        try:\n            result = process_session(sf['data_file'], sf['me_file'], idx)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"\\n  ERROR processing session {idx} ({sf['animal_date']}): {e}\")\n            import traceback\n            traceback.print_exc()\n            continue\n\n        if result is None:\n            continue\n\n        all_neural.append(result['neural'])\n        all_input.append(result['input'])\n        all_output.append(result['output'])\n        all_animals.append(result['animal'])\n        all_dates.append(result['date'])\n        all_region_labels.append(result['region_labels'])\n\n    n_sessions = len(all_neural)\n    print(f\"\\n=== Successfully processed {n_sessions} sessions ===\")\n\n    if n_sessions == 0:\n        print(\"ERROR: No sessions processed successfully\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Build subject index ---\n    unique_subjects = sorted(set(all_animals))\n    subject_idx = np.array([unique_subjects.index(a) for a in all_animals], dtype=np.int64)\n\n    # --- Build brain region index ---\n    all_regions_flat = set()\n    for labels in all_region_labels:\n        all_regions_flat.update(labels)\n    brain_regions = sorted(all_regions_flat)\n\n    brain_region_idx = []\n    for labels in all_region_labels:\n        idx_arr = np.array([brain_regions.index(l) for l in labels], dtype=np.int64)\n        brain_region_idx.append(idx_arr)\n\n    # --- Build output ---\n    data = {\n        'neural': all_neural,\n        'input': all_input,\n        'output': all_output,\n        'subjects': unique_subjects,\n        'subject_idx': subject_idx,\n        'brain_regions': brain_regions,\n        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx,\n        'input_names': ['time_from_go_cue'],\n        'output_names': [\n            'lick_direction',\n            'behavioral_context',\n            'outcome',\n            'tongue_velocity',\n            'paw_velocity',\n            'motion_energy',\n        ],\n        'output_values': [\n            ['left', 'right'],\n            ['WC', 'DR'],\n            ['incorrect', 'correct'],\n            ['low', 'high'],\n            ['low', 'high'],\n            ['low', 'high'],\n        ],\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description': 'Mice perform delayed-response (DR) and water-cued (WC) directional licking tasks with ALM recordings',\n            'time_bin_size': DT * 1000,  # in ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'Go cue onset',\n            'off_start': TMIN,  # -2.5 s before go cue\n            'off_end': TMAX,  # +2.5 s after go cue\n            'paper': 'Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nature Neuroscience 2024',\n            'recording_region': 'ALM (anterior lateral motor cortex)',\n            'n_sessions': n_sessions,\n            'sessions': [f\"{a}_{d}\" for a, d in zip(all_animals, all_dates)],\n            'smooth_ms': SMOOTH_MS,\n            'low_fr_threshold': LOW_FR,\n        },\n    }\n\n    # Save\n    with open(output_file, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=4)\n\n    print(f\"\\nSaved converted data to {output_file}\")\n    print(f\"  Sessions: {n_sessions}\")\n    print(f\"  Subjects: {unique_subjects}\")\n    print(f\"  Brain regions: {brain_regions}\")\n    total_trials = sum(len(s) for s in all_neural)\n    total_neurons = sum(s[0].shape[0] for s in all_neural if len(s) > 0)\n    print(f\"  Total trials: {total_trials}\")\n    print(f\"  Total neurons: {total_neurons}\")\n\n    return data\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    import argparse\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert neural data to decoder format.')\n    parser.add_argument('--max-sessions', type=int, default=None,\n                        help='Max number of sessions to process (for testing)')\n    parser.add_argument('--output', type=str, default='converted_data.pkl',\n                        help='Output pickle file path')\n    parser.add_argument('--data-dirs', type=str, nargs='+', default=DATA_DIRS,\n                        help='Data directories to search')\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    convert_data(\n        data_dirs=args.data_dirs,\n        max_sessions=args.max_sessions,\n        output_file=args.output,\n    )\n","numLines":1151,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1151}}}

structure:\\n\\n```python\\ndata = {\\n    'neural': [  # list of sessions\\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # shape: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\\n            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # trial 2\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        [  # list of trials for session 2\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'input': [  # list of sessions\\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\\n            input_data,  # stimulus/task variables, shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input)\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'output': [  # list of sessions\\n        [  # list of trials for session 1\\n            output_data,  # behavioral response, shape (n_output, n_timepoints) or (n_output)\\n            ...\\n        ],\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'subjects': list of str, # ids of all subjects, length n_subjects\\n    'subject_idx': shape (n_sessions,), # index into subjects for each session\\n\\n    'brain_regions': list of str, # names of the brain regions recorded from, e.g. ALM, V1\\n    'brain_region_idx': [ # list of sessions\\n        neuron_region_idx, # shape: (n_neurons,)\\n    ], # index into `brain_regions` for each neuron\\n\\n    'input_names': list of str, # names for each input, of length n_input\\n    'output_names': list of str, # names of each output, of length n_output\\n    'output_values': [ # list of length n_output\\n        [ output_val0, ... ], # list of str of length d_output[0], name of each output value\\n        ...\\n    ],\\n\\n    'metadata': {\\n        'task_description': str, # description of output tasks\\n        'time_bin_size': float, # length of time bin in ms\\n        'temporal_alignment_event': str, # description of alignment event\\n        'off_start': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to start of trial in seconds, None if N/A\\n        'off_end': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to end of trial in seconds, None if N/A\\n        ...\\n    }\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### Key Format Requirements\\n\\n- **neural**: Neural activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\\n  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\\n  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \\n  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\\n\\n- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\\n  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\\n  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\\n    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\\n    - audio: frequency, amplitude\\n    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\\n  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \\n  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\\n  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \\n\\n- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\\n  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\\n  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \\n  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\\n  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\\n  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \\n\\n- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\\n- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\\n  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\\n\\n- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\\n- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\\n\\n- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\\n  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \\n\\n- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\\n  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\\n- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\\n  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\\n\\n- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \\n  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\\n  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\\n  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \\n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\\n\\n## Decoder Reference\\n\\n- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\\n- Optional arguments:\\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\\n- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\\n\\n### Purpose\\n\\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\\n1. Data structure is correct - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \\n2. Dimensions are consistent - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \\n3. Values are sensible - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \\n4. Decoder can successfully train and predict\\n5. Poor performance indicates data formatting issues that need investigation\\n\\n### Consistency requirements\\n\\nYour processing and formatting must **match the reference paper and code** with respect to:\\n- Loading of data\\n- Temporal alignment of different time series streams (neural, inputs, outputs)\\n- Processing of neural, input, and output data streams\\n- Curation of data: filtering of low-quality neurons, trials, sessions, and mice.\\n\\nTo check consistency, you must compare statistics available in the reference paper and your converted dataset. \\n\\nWhenever possible, invent **SANITY CHECKS** that your loading and processing matches the reference paper and code. \\n\\nDiscrepancies are only allowed if required by the Decoder Input and Decoder Output specifications above. \\n\\nCarefully verify your work after every step. When you find mistakes, document and fix them immediately. Be critical of results **after every step of processing**. \\n\\nYou will be assessed on whether the decisions you make on how to load, filter, process, align, reformat, and save the data are reasonable and match the reference paper and code and these instructions. During testing, the reference version of `train_decoder.py` will be run on your converted data. Data statistics and decoder accuracy will be compared to those achieved by human-written conversion code. \\n\\n## Success Criteria\\n\\nA successful conversion should:\\n1.
